### USER FACTS

* **Identity & Demographics**: Caroline is a transgender woman who was born circa 1995 (age 28 in 2023). She transitioned around 2020. She moved from her home country of Sweden to her current location around 2019.
* **Relationships**: Caroline has a supportive network of friends and family, including a close group of friends she has known since 2019. Her closest friend is Melanie (married since ~2018, mother of three). Caroline has experienced a difficult breakup in her past. She is currently single and planning to adopt and raise children as a single parent.
* **Pets**: She owns a pet guinea pig named Oscar who loves eating vegetables, particularly parsley.
* **Key Possessions**: A sentimental necklace featuring a cross and a heart gifted by her grandmother in Sweden, and a hand-painted bowl gifted by a friend for her 18th birthday.
* **Personal Preferences**: Her favorite color is blue because of its relaxing effect. Her favorite book is *Becoming Nicole* by Amy Ellis Nutt.
* **Hobbies & Creative Talents**: 
  * **Art**: Caroline has been creating art since approximately 2012 (age 17), specializing in painting, drawing, and stained glass. Notable paintings include *Embracing Identity*, a self-portrait with a blue face, and a sunset scene. She also designed a stained glass window for a local church symbolizing her transition journey.
  * **Music**: She has played the acoustic guitar since circa 2018; her favorite and most meaningful song to play is 'Brave' by Sara Bareilles. She is also learning to play the piano.
  * **Recreation**: She enjoyed horseback riding with her father during childhood. She is open to trying pottery.
* **Career & Advocacy**: Caroline is actively seeking career opportunities in counseling and mental health to support transgender individuals. She is a highly active LGBTQ+ advocate, public speaker, youth center volunteer, and mentor to a transgender teenager.

* **Recreational Activity Pattern**:
  * **Rule**: Fixed 12-day alternation cycle between State A and State B.

* **Volunteer Activity Pattern**:
  * **Rule**: Weekly 7-day cycle.
  * **Schedule**: Tuesday through Friday (4 days) is dedicated to 'Connected LGBTQ Activists'. Saturday through Monday (3 days) is dedicated to 'mentoring LGBTQ youth'.

  * **Execution**: Repeats every 7 days without variation.

* **2018**: Began playing the acoustic guitar.
* **2019**: Moved from Sweden and established a core, supportive group of friends.
* **2020**: Began her gender transition.
* **2022**: Attended a Pride festival with her close friend Melanie.
* **2023**:

  * **September**: Went biking with friends (September 9-10) and organized a local LGBTQ+ art show to showcase her paintings and support other queer artists.

* **Personal Preferences**: Her favorite color is blue because of its relaxing effect. Her favorite book is *Becoming Nicole* by Amy Ellis Nutt.

  * **Art**: Caroline has been creating art since approximately 2012 (age 17), specializing in painting, drawing, and stained glass. Notable paintings include *Embracing Identity*, a self-portrait with a blue face, and a sunset scene. She also designed a stained glass window for a local church symbolizing her transition journey.

* **Career & Advocacy**: Caroline is actively seeking career opportunities in counseling and mental health to support transgender individuals. She is a highly active LGBTQ+ advocate, public speaker, youth center volunteer, and mentor to a transgender teenager.

  * **Recreation**: She enjoyed horseback riding with her father during childhood. She is open to trying pottery.
* **Career & Advocacy**: Caroline is actively seeking career opportunities in counseling and mental health to support transgender individuals. She is a highly active LGBTQ+ advocate, public speaker, youth center volunteer, and mentor to a transgender teenager.

### USER FACTS

* **Identity & Demographics**: Caroline is a transgender woman who was born circa 1995 (age 28 in 2023). She transitioned around 2020. She moved from her home country of Sweden to her current location around 2019.
* **Relationships**: Caroline has a supportive network of friends and family, including a close group of friends she has known since 2019. Her closest friend is Melanie (married since ~2018, mother of three). Caroline has experienced a difficult breakup in her past. She is currently single and planning to adopt and raise children as a single parent.
* **Pets**: She owns a pet guinea pig named Oscar who loves eating vegetables, particularly parsley.
* **Key Possessions**: A sentimental necklace featuring a cross and a heart gifted by her grandmother in Sweden, and a hand-painted bowl gifted by a friend for her 18th birthday.
* **Personal Preferences**: Her favorite color is blue because of its relaxing effect. Her favorite book is *Becoming Nicole* by Amy Ellis Nutt.
* **Hobbies & Creative Talents**: 
  * **Art**: Caroline has been creating art since approximately 2012 (age 17), specializing in painting, drawing, and stained glass. Notable paintings include *Embracing Identity*, a self-portrait with a blue face, and a sunset scene. She also designed a stained glass window for a local church symbolizing her transition journey.
  * **Music**: She has played the acoustic guitar since circa 2018; her favorite and most meaningful song to play is 'Brave' by Sara Bareilles. She is also learning to play the piano.
  * **Recreation**: She enjoyed horseback riding with her father during childhood. She is open to trying pottery.
* **Career & Advocacy**: Caroline is actively seeking career opportunities in counseling and mental health to support transgender individuals. She is a highly active LGBTQ+ advocate, public speaker, youth center volunteer, and mentor to a transgender teenager.

Her closest friend is Melanie (married since ~2018, mother of three). Caroline has experienced a difficult breakup in her past. She is currently single and planning to adopt and raise children as a single parent.

* **Key Possessions**: A sentimental necklace featuring a cross and a heart gifted by her grandmother in Sweden, and a hand-painted bowl gifted by a friend for her 18th birthday.
* **Personal Preferences**: Her favorite color is blue because of its relaxing effect. Her favorite book is *Becoming Nicole* by Amy Ellis Nutt.
* **Hobbies & Creative Talents**: 
  * **Art**: Caroline has been creating art since approximately 2012 (age 17), specializing in painting, drawing, and stained glass. Notable paintings include *Embracing Identity*, a self-portrait with a blue face, and a sunset scene. She also designed a stained glass window for a local church symbolizing her transition journey.
  * **Music**: She has played the acoustic guitar since circa 2018; her favorite and most meaningful song to play is 'Brave' by Sara Bareilles. She is also learning to play the piano.
  * **Recreation**: She enjoyed horseback riding with her father during childhood. She is open to trying pottery.
* **Career & Advocacy**: Caroline is actively seeking career opportunities in counseling and mental health to support transgender individuals. She is a highly active LGBTQ+ advocate, public speaker, youth center volunteer, and mentor to a transgender teenager.
